                              - link "Spell Totem" [ref=f1262e325] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/spell-totem
                              - text: have INSANE bonuses granting 6% more damage per remaining chain ( about 30 chains currently or 180% more damage) and 120% cast speed with 6 totems up. For all stages of the guide try to have your
                              - link "Spell Totem" [ref=f1262e330] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/spell-totem
                              - text: around level 23, this is the minimum level for +3 totem limit while costing the least amount of mana. You can give a couple additional levels for additional totem tankiness but keep this in mind.
                            - paragraph [ref=f1262e334]:
                              - text: READ
                              - generic [ref=f1262e335]: Ancestral Bond
                              - text: . This makes it so our totems cost spirit instead of power/endurance charges.
                            - paragraph [ref=f1262e340]:
                              - strong [ref=f1262e341]: Setups are available for all budget levels, this build does NOT require a high budget in any way to completely crush all content in the game.
                            - paragraph [ref=f1262e342]:
                              - strong [ref=f1262e343]: LIVE RIGHT NOW
                              - text: "-"
                              - link "Twitch" [ref=f1262e344] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: https://twitch.tv/pathofky
                            - paragraph [ref=f1262e345]:
                              - text: 0.5.5 Starter Video -
                              - link "Youtube" [ref=f1262e346] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: https://youtu.be/t6HXjq8Wnsg
                            - paragraph [ref=f1262e347]: Make sure to lock your weapon and sceptre to both weapon sets and have your sets set correctly in both your passive tree and gems!!!
                        - generic [ref=f1262e348]:
                          - heading "Strengths and Weaknesses" [level=2] [ref=f1262e352]
                          - generic [ref=f1262e355]:
                            - list [ref=f1262e362]:
                              - listitem [ref=f1262e363]: Auto-Targeting Offscreen Clear
                              - listitem [ref=f1262e364]: 30 Chains
                              - listitem [ref=f1262e365]: No Casting Slowdown
                              - listitem [ref=f1262e366]: 1-Button & Low Input
                            - list [ref=f1262e373]:
                              - listitem [ref=f1262e374]:
                                - text: Needs around 5k mana and a
                                - link "Kurgal's Gaze" [ref=f1262e376] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/abyssal-eyes/kurgals-gaze
                                - text: before feeling tanky
                        - generic [ref=f1262e380]:
                          - generic [ref=f1262e381]:
                            - heading "Build Variants" [level=2] [ref=f1262e385]
                            - tablist [ref=f1262e389]:
                              - tablist [ref=f1262e391]:
                                - 'tab "Act 1 {1}" [selected] [ref=f1262e392] [cursor=pointer]'
                                - 'tab "Act 2 {2}" [ref=f1262e395] [cursor=pointer]'
                                - 'tab "Act 3 {3}" [ref=f1262e397] [cursor=pointer]'
                                - 'tab "Act 4 {4}" [ref=f1262e399] [cursor=pointer]'
                                - 'tab "Interludes+ {5}" [ref=f1262e401] [cursor=pointer]'
                                - tab "Leaguestart Absolute Minimum Entry (lvl 70 ish)" [ref=f1262e403] [cursor=pointer]
                                - tab "Leaguestart Absolute Minimum Entry Step 2" [ref=f1262e405] [cursor=pointer]
                                - tab "SUPER LOW BUDGET ( Warrior Variant Jewel )" [ref=f1262e407] [cursor=pointer]
                                - tab "Low Budget (Templar Jewel)" [ref=f1262e409] [cursor=pointer]
                                - tab "Medium Budget" [ref=f1262e411] [cursor=pointer]
                                - tab "Medium Budget Step 2" [ref=f1262e413] [cursor=pointer]
                                - tab "Besos-Maxing Step 1 [ ~500-600 Div ish ]" [ref=f1262e415] [cursor=pointer]
                                - tab "Besos-Maxing Step 2 [ 1100 Div ish ]" [ref=f1262e417] [cursor=pointer]
                                - tab "Besos-Maxing Step 3 [ ~1800div ish ]" [ref=f1262e419] [cursor=pointer]
                                - tab "Elon-Maxing Step 1 [ 3300div idk ]" [ref=f1262e421] [cursor=pointer]
                                - tab "The Pinnacle [ Mirror-Tier ]" [ref=f1262e423] [cursor=pointer]
                                - tab "Acts Twink Spark Leveling Setup" [ref=f1262e425] [cursor=pointer]
                            - generic [ref=f1262e433]:
                              - paragraph [ref=f1262e434]:
                                - text: Starter Video -
                                - link "Youtube" [ref=f1262e435] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: https://youtu.be/t6HXjq8Wnsg
                              - paragraph [ref=f1262e436]:
                                - text: Stream -
                                - link "Twitch" [ref=f1262e437] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: https://twitch.tv/pathofky
                              - paragraph [ref=f1262e438]:
                                - text: Unfortunately you cannot level as
                                - link "Arc" [ref=f1262e440] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/arc
                                - link "Spell Totem" [ref=f1262e445] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/spell-totem
                                - text: as it requires a lot of passive points to make it to the
                                - generic [ref=f1262e449]: Ancestral Bond
                                - text: keystone which this build requires to function.
                              - paragraph [ref=f1262e454]: Instead we play as a Grenade Mercenary with the intent to swap to arc totems around level 65-70ish. There is unfortunately no way around this. You can play any leveling build you would like, but I have the setup I will be using laid out here.
                              - paragraph [ref=f1262e455]:
                                - text: Here you will use
                                - link "Explosive Grenade" [ref=f1262e457] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/explosive-grenade
                                - link "Flash Grenade" [ref=f1262e462] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/flash-grenade
                                - link "Gas Grenade" [ref=f1262e467] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/gas-grenade
                                - link "Oil Grenade" [ref=f1262e472] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/oil-grenade
                                - link "Cluster Grenade" [ref=f1262e477] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/cluster-grenade
                                - text: ", to speedrun through campaign."
                              - paragraph [ref=f1262e481]:
                                - text: "Leveling POB:"
                                - link "https://pobb.in/DttTF88yOjkA" [ref=f1262e482] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: https://pobb.in/DttTF88yOjkA
                              - heading "How to Play:" [level=3] [ref=f1262e483]
                              - heading "Act 1" [level=4] [ref=f1262e484]
                              - paragraph [ref=f1262e485]:
                                - text: Spend your first support gem on
                                - link "Multishot I" [ref=f1262e487] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/multishot-i
                                - text: for your
                                - link "Explosive Grenade" [ref=f1262e492] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/explosive-grenade
                                - text: followed by
                                - link "Multishot I" [ref=f1262e497] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/multishot-i
                                - text: for
                                - link "Flash Grenade" [ref=f1262e502] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/flash-grenade
                                - text: . You should fire your
                                - link "Explosive Grenade" [ref=f1262e507] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/explosive-grenade
                                - text: far ahead of yourself and aim for the mobs to run into them as you are running away. This can take some getting used to.
                              - paragraph [ref=f1262e511]:
                                - text: For general clear you will be using primarily
                                - link "Explosive Grenade" [ref=f1262e513] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/explosive-grenade
                                - text: ", cast it the moment you see a pack far ahead of you. This will be a long delay for now but it gets shorter and shorter as we progress."
                                - link "Flash Grenade" [ref=f1262e518] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/flash-grenade
                                - text: can be used for a more instant but less potent burst of damage. This also causes huge stun-buildup so you should use it on cd for bosses to build stun bar.
                              - paragraph [ref=f1262e522]:
                                - text: Try to cut a level 5 skill gem before the final boss for
                                - link "Gas Grenade" [ref=f1262e524] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/gas-grenade
                                - text: . Your priority should loosely be
                                - link "Frost Bomb" [ref=f1262e529] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/frost-bomb
                                - text: ( for exposure as needed ) >
                                - link "Explosive Grenade" [ref=f1262e534] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/explosive-grenade
                                - text: ">"
                                - link "Gas Grenade" [ref=f1262e539] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/gas-grenade
                                - text: ">"
                                - link "Flash Grenade" [ref=f1262e544] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/flash-grenade
                                - text: ( for stun buildup. ) As long as the mob is ingited
                                - link "Gas Grenade" [ref=f1262e549] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/gas-grenade
                                - text: will pop instantly.
                              - paragraph [ref=f1262e553]:
                                - strong [ref=f1262e554]: "Focus on +# to # added X damage on your crossbow and both rings. It is INCREDIBLY important to keep this value as high as possible as it is the baseline for all your skills damage."
                                - text: Check Vendors constantly to keep your weapon and rings up to date.
                              - paragraph [ref=f1262e555]: For Armour Pieces look to use Either Armour or Armour/Evasion Hyrbid Pieces with:. +# to Maximum Life. +#% Increased Cold/Fire/Lightning Resistnace. High Total Aarmour/Evasion Rating Value

                        - generic [ref=f1262e1234]:
                          - heading "How it Plays" [level=2] [ref=f1262e1238]
                          - generic [ref=f1262e1245]:
                            - heading "FAQ" [level=3] [ref=f1262e1246]
                            - paragraph [ref=f1262e1247]:
                              - text: Its VERY important for single target that you have your weapon set passives set correctly in both your passive tree and gem assignments.
                              - strong [ref=f1262e1248]: Also MAKE SURE your last cast is either a totem or mana tempest so that you are resting in set 1 ( archmage ).
                            - heading "When do I swap?" [level=4] [ref=f1262e1249]
                            - paragraph [ref=f1262e1250]: Swap when you have a gear set prepared to put you around 3.5k mana and you are AT MINIMUM level 70. BOTH OF THESE TOGETHER ARE REQUIRED not 1 or the other. Mana regen on rings and spirit on both your chest and amulet are very important aswell.
                            - heading "Upgrade order?" [level=4] [ref=f1262e1251]
                            - paragraph [ref=f1262e1252]: Buy Low Basic Stater Set of Gears -> !gring, !wand -> Buy/Semi-Craft a Cheap Medium Budget Amulet / Scepter / Helm / Chest / Boots / Gloves !gamulet -> !gbody -> !ghelm -> !ggloves -> !fork -> Mageblood -> !ggbody -> !gsceptre
